- root cause (CONFIRMED, concrete file/line): the OpenCode PO story-creation surface is a thin stub with no canonical structured story-block template. `.opencode/agents/po.md` (19 lines; no field template) and `.opencode/commands/intake.md` (19-line dispatch-only stub ending "STOP") carry no structured story-block contract, so the OpenCode PO emits prose (`### US-0001` H3, `**Priority:**`, `**Release:**`, `**Acceptance criteria**` bullets, no `Status`) instead of the canonical structured block. Canonical structured reference is `## US-0161` at `docs/product/backlog.md` lines 6143-6177 (intake cited 6098-6133; that citation has drifted - use the live lines). Cursor surfaces (`.cursor/agents/po.mdc` 143 lines; `.cursor/commands/intake.md` 351 lines with the full US-0051 persistence contract) carry the template, so Cursor-created stories are structured and OpenCode-created ones are prose. The "shared story writer" is the PO LLM role; no separate Python story-writer script exists (greps `story.?writer|write_story|backlog_block|story_block|build_story` return none); one-off backfill scripts are not the live writer. Scope is OpenCode-only (AC-2: Cursor/Pi are not promised user outcomes).
- AC-4 contract-test assertion list (LOCKED in discovery, positive canonical shape): (1) canonical H2 heading `## US-#### - <Title>` (not H3 `###`, not `# User Story`); (2) `- user_visible:`; (3) `- Title:`; (4) `- Summary:`; (5) `- Priority: P<n>`; (6) `- Status:` (the operator's headline missing field, MUST be present); (7) `- Acceptance:` header with at least one structured `  - [ ] AC-n:` bullet. Plus the shape discriminator (8): the block must NOT use the prose markers `**Priority:**`, `**Release:**`, or `**Acceptance criteria**` (fields are `- <field>:` lines, not bold-label prose). Deliberately NOT over-locked to intake-specific fields (intake_run_id, writer_id, topic_coverage) so the contract stays about shape, not provenance.
- remediation direction (direction only; no code edit by PO): **Option A (recommended)** restore the canonical structured story-block template on the OpenCode host story-creation surface (OpenCode PO command/agent surface), minimal + additive, without weakening the existing canonical block (`US-0161`) and without importing remote `US-0001`..`US-0004`; pair the template restoration with the AC-4 contract test. **Option B (rejected here)** defer to a cross-host template-parity track only if it later proves the canonical template is shared and the defect is a sync failure; current evidence indicates an OpenCode-specific stub, so A is the bounded remediation.
- scope locks: OpenCode-only; discovery-only; no agent-map edit (`.opencode/agents/*`, `template/**` are do-not-edit for this phase); no production code; no remote import; no US-0161 weakening/reopen; no AC tick; no commit/push/publish; no `/auto` or child spawn.
